Then `### 3.2 The row series is closed at forty`, before `토론 패스` made it forty-one; `### 3.2 The row series is closed at forty` was also the count on a line of work where `토론 패스` arrived on top of thirty-nine before `중단 리포트 건너뜀`, and the two lines were joined afterwards. A heading that states a count states a falsehood the moment the count moves, and it has moved twenty-two times; every one of those spellings is kept here rather than replaced so a citation written against any of them still lands.

**A writer that needs a kind not on this list extends this definition; it does not improvise one.**

**The thirty-third, `재계획`, voids a sibling's plan after another segment's re-convergence moved the binding surface.** It is written by `act --kind replan --segment <형제> -- 재수렴=<스테이지>#<실행 버전>`, a bookkeeping kind graded `읽기`; argv carries the cite key and nothing else (any other key is exit 2 with no row), and `회차` is the gate's count. The gate writes it only when the sibling has a `segment` row and has not ended, no stage of it is live, it holds an implement row with `plan_sha256` above the cited row, the cite names exactly one gate-written `종류=reconverge` row reading `재수렴 판정=재설계 필요` on another segment, no earlier `재계획` row pairs the sibling with that cite, the round is at most two, and no held-class question (`사람-결정` or `시각-승인`) holds the sibling — an open one, or one a person closed `거부` or `무효` that no row has consumed yet; otherwise exit 3 and nothing is written, and after the run's end every one is refused. It moves `<형제>.plan.md` aside first and then writes the row, which resets the plan-void rule the way a `종류=reconverge` row does. The snapshot's `replan_due[]` lists the siblings this applies to with `state` (`가능` · `살아 있음` · `상한` · `보류`) from the same evaluation, leaving out a sibling past the round cap that a person has decided since the cited re-convergence — a `사람-결정` on it issued after that row and consumed as `결정=채택` or `결정=자유 입력 소비`. No termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector.

**A `blocked` row with `스코프=cone` anchored on a segment reaches the router through the snapshot's `cone_blocks[]`, not `blocked[]`.** That key is a projection of the run pane's fold (`cc_cone_blocked`): one element per subject and `사유`, the last row kept, `{subject, state, cause, reason, observed}` with `state` `held` or `unresolved` and `observed` `null` when `관측` is absent. A segment-anchored cone row is never closed by a row; it leaves the key once a `segment` row of that segment that is not `park` lands after it. The key is outside the default `--fields` projection and `H`, and no termination condition, status line or run verdict reads it.

An approval and a deferred review obligation are **non-terminal states with their own lifecycle**, which is why they are series of their own rather than an overloaded `자율 승인` or `blocked`.

**`교대 기동` is the record of a successor's start itself**, written immediately before it, and it is what the shift ordinal is counted from; `handoff` and the authorisation row do not say a successor started. Its `서수` is the number of the shift being launched, which is deliberately not the same quantity as the `교대` seat every row carries: the seat says who did the launching.

**`컨텍스트` is the OUTGOING shift's last-turn context — read + creation + input — and not the launcher's.** Launches are serial, so the shift being replaced is `서수` minus one, and the process writing this row is the seat that launched both. A kickoff launch has no outgoing shift, and a launch whose predecessor's transcript could not be resolved, or resolved but holds no usage line to score (a shift that died on its first request), has no honest number; both carry `-`, which is a third state and is not read as zero. A row without the field at all was written before the field existed.

**`경계 억제` records a suppression, which is frequent and otherwise silent.** It may not be written to `blocked`, because the obligation boundary reads `blocked` — **the load-bearing property is a property of the NAME**: no boundary reads it. `크레딧 잔량` is on the row because the suppression is bounded.

**`pace` records what the run saw of a pacing verdict that lives outside the run.** It is written by the gate on the record path of `act` and `exec` — immediately after the `자율 승인` row, and never by `snapshot`, which is a read and stays one — and only when the verdict differs from the last `pace` row of THIS run's ledger, or when the run has none yet; the basis is run-scoped so the 8-row ancestry window carries a `pace` row. An absent, unreadable, foreign-schema or stale state (older than three sensor periods, 180s) is written as `판정=(미상)` with `기준 틱=-` and `관측=-` rather than skipped — the row says the gate looked and found no verdict, which a reader has to tell apart from the gate never looking. **Two closed token sets, never mixed**: `판정` and `이전` take the sensor's verdict vocabulary (`가속` · `유지` · `제동` · `(미상)`), and `사유` takes the sensor's reason vocabulary (`brake` · `idle` · `lanes-below-target` · `default` · `tracker-skip` · `truncated` · `(미상)`); a dispatch refusal clause (`lane` · `window` · `burn` · `brake`) is a third vocabulary and belongs to `fleet.sh`'s own refusal log, not to this row. With `pace` the series counts twenty; `checks` below makes it twenty-one, and `stage-lease` and `stage-wait` make it twenty-three.

**The twenty-first exists because CI is a non-terminal state with a lifecycle of its own, and every candidate series is wired to a termination condition.** `checks` records what a run-scope CI poller saw on a segment's PR. Reusing `blocked` (condition 5), `승인` (condition 2) or `리뷰 의무` (condition 9) would mean the waiting itself stops the run from ever finishing; `stage-result` is a fact about a stage that terminated and a CI run is not a stage — it has no exit code, no session id and no artifact predicate; `자율 승인` is a decision the run MADE and waiting is not a decision. All five candidates fall the same way, and `대기 → 통과|실패|미등록|판정 불가` is exactly the shape this section already accepted twice. **The identity of a row is the triple `(PR, head sha, 재실행 회차)`, not the PR** — a force-push starts a new CI lifecycle and must not read as a continuation of the previous one, and so does a rerun GitHub accepted — and **a row is written only on a TRANSITION** of (`상태`, `진행 중` > 0), because a row per poll would leave forty identical `대기` rows behind one forty-minute wait. The round `g` is the one `cc_ci_round` reads from this ledger (`CI 처분` below), on every poll. A new round's key is made on the poll after `g` rose and inherits no previous value, so its first observation is always written; the start-up seeding of the state table reaches only the keys that existed at start-up. A `checks` row carries `재실행 회차` and `진행 중` (the number of `pending` lines in the same `gh pr checks` output); a row without them reads as round 0 with nothing pending. **The observer is `checks.sh` and the writer is the gate's `gate_drain_checks`**: the poller is a detached process and therefore not this ledger's writer, so it appends to `$RUN_DIR/checks.observed` and the gate transcribes on its next act. **No termination clause attaches to this series** — a run whose last reading was `대기` simply ends, and the morning report says the CI wait was left open, which is the same disposition an open query already gets. **It is not in the progress vector**, and the number of lines to change for that is zero: `gate_progress_vector` selects series positively by name, so a new one is outside it by construction. Adding a `실패` transition as a component would be the opposite edit and a harmful one — the vector feeds the stagnation boundary, so a CI failure counted as progress would reset the counter that exists to notice nothing is moving.

**The thirty-eighth exists because a rerun and a CI fix spend budgets the run has to count from its own record, and `checks` records what CI saw, not what the run did about it.** `CI 처분` is a record series outside the progress vector, on the same reasoning as `checks`: no termination condition reads it, and a rerun is not progress. Its fields are `세그먼트`, `PR`, `head sha`, `처분` (`재실행` \| `수정` \| `합류` \| `라벨`), `상태` (`요청` \| `수락` \| `실패`), `회차`, `실행`, `워크플로`, `실패 체크`, `체크`, `라벨`, `기본 브랜치`, `응답`, `실행 주체` (on a person's round only, value `사람`), `근거` (on a person's round only, `중단 답 줄 <n>`) and `관측`, clipped to the row cap. **Three writers**: the gate's rerun act `act --kind ci-rerun` writes `처분=재실행` — a `요청` row before its POST, then a `수락` row or a closing `실패` row of the same `회차`; on a person's `재시도` answer to this head's rerun park it sends no POST and writes a `요청` row and a `수락` row of the same `회차` together, with `실행=-`, `워크플로=-`, `응답=-`, `실행 주체=사람` and `근거=중단 답 줄 <n>` — the gate's label act `act --kind label-ack` writes `처분=라벨` the same way around its label POST, carrying the acknowledged `체크` and the `라벨` it put on, and the implement dispatch guard writes `처분=수정` with `head sha`, on `act` only and only when `(세그먼트, head sha)` has no such row yet, and `처분=합류` for a `CI 합류:` dispatch the same way; `cc_ci_round` does not count `합류` rows. **The round is one state machine over `(세그먼트, head sha)`, and every reader reads it through one function**, `liveness.sh`'s `cc_ci_round <원장> <세그먼트> <head sha>`: a rerun round and a label round are both rounds, numbered by one counter — a new `요청` takes the pair's highest `회차` + 1 (`max`), whatever its `처분`; `u` is the number of `처분=재실행 상태=요청` rows (the spent rerun budget — a `요청` that died before its closing row still spent it; a label round never counts), `a` is the state of the highest `회차` (`없음` \| `열림` \| `수락` \| `실패`), `ad` is that round's `처분`, `ra` is the state of the highest `처분=재실행` round (`없음` when there is none — the rerun act reads its own open or failed request from it), `lab` is 1 when the pair has a `처분=라벨` row, `g` is the highest `회차` among the `상태=수락` rows or 0 — a round that is open or closed by `실패` raises nothing, so what CI showed during it stays the previous round's — and `f` is the number of distinct `head sha` among the segment's `처분=수정` rows, read beside whether `(세그먼트, head sha)` has one. The four readers are the CI layer of `다음`, the merge check, the poller's key and the relay's verdict reader. **The merge check picks its verdict the way the CI layer does** — the latest `checks` row of `(seg, H)` with `재실행 회차` = `g`, so a row of another head written after it does not hide it — and refuses while the round is open, at `g` ≥ 1 unless that row reads `통과`, and at `g` = 0 on `실패` as before, `필수 집합=판정 불가` still exempt — except on a CI join target with a `cycle` row, where round 0 merges only on `통과` or a `미등록` past the grace, and no row, `대기`, `실패` whatever its required set, `판정 불가`, a `미등록` within the grace and a wait past the cap are refused. On a routed run the refusal is exit 3 naming `다음` and no park cell; a run without a routing record and the fixed-graph driver keep the `CI실패` park. The relay's reader takes the segment worktree's tip as H — the newest verdict's own sha only when that tip does not resolve — and counts only round `g` of it, reading `실패` with `진행 중` > 0, and no verdict of the current round, as `대기`. **The relay wakes on a CI segment (`ci:<seg>`) when its wait ended**: `통과` or a settled `실패` (clock: the verdict's `관측`), round 0's `미등록` past the grace (clock: `관측` + grace), or the wait cap (clock: the wait clock + cap); no verdict, `대기`, a failure still running, `판정 불가` and a `미등록` within the grace wake nothing before the cap. **It wakes on a stage of the wait set (`ce:<seg>`) when E holds for it**, clock the round-0 failure's `관측`. **Each such condition is consumed once**: when the ledger's last `교대 기동` row stands below the condition's evidence row and was written at or after its wake time, the shift that launched on it has seen it, and it does not wake again; evidence still in a poller file is never consumed. **The relay's wait and its reads only read the ledger; the shift launch it calls drains the poller's verdicts into the ledger (`gate_drain_checks`) just before it appends the `교대 기동` row**, inside or outside the relay loop, so the woken shift's snapshot already carries the verdict that woke it.

**The twenty-fourth exists because a run's inventory baseline has to leave a trace the morning can enumerate, and `blocked` would hold condition 5.** `인벤토리 기준선` is written once per publish, by the entry whose publish succeeded. **The writer is the driver's `rundir_init`**: through `gate_append` when the gate calls it, through `ledger_row` outside the gate. A success that could not be recorded — no ledger yet, or a failed row write — is left in `inventory.unrecorded` and the next entry writes the row. Like `run` it is a **preamble row**: it is written ahead of any verb, `plan` included, and is not a row the verb itself writes. `이전 지문` equal to `지문` means the content did not change, not that the baseline was not taken again. A marker row carries `-` in both fingerprints, so that equality says nothing about a change of root; a change of root shows only in `판정 루트`.

**The twenty-sixth exists because a person's "the limit has cleared" has to reach a shift the seat does not route.** `한도 해제` is written by `act --kind limit-cleared`, and **by the seat only**: a caller with its own shift ordinal, a stage, or a caller carrying `CC_PIPELINE_SHIFT_ID` is refused with exit 3 and no row, judged in the order the seat arm of the context cap uses. Argv carries `근거` — a summary of what the person said, clipped to the row cap — and nothing else; any other key, `레인` and `기록 시각` included, is exit 2 with no row, because the lane and the time this row testifies to are the gate's. **It decides nothing**: no termination condition reads it, it is outside the progress vector, and nothing is notified from it — it moves the chain tip and no more, which is why `blocked`, wired to condition 5, could not carry it. Its one reader is a shift's re-attach rule, which compares its ledger line with a cut stage's `stage-result` line; a re-attached attempt that is cut again lands a later `stage-result` row, so **each row is spent per cut** and never releases the same cut twice.

**The twenty-seventh exists because a person's answer to a halt record has to reach a shift the seat does not route.** `중단 답` is written by `act --kind halt-answer`, by the seat only, on the same terms as `한도 해제` — with one exception for a shift: an `implement-unattended` record whose `스텝` (up to its first ` — `) carries `CFI-U3 BT-STOP`, answered `재수렴`, when `## 인가` holds a `자동 채택` row of class `구속-이탈`. The gate stamps that row `답한 주체=교대` and prefixes its `근거` with `자동 채택(구속-이탈) — `; a shift that names `답한 주체` itself, or misses any condition, is refused (exit 3), and a stage never writes the row. **`답한 주체` tells the writers apart**: `사람`, `좌석` and `교대`; any other value is exit 2. **The visual publish stage's three question sites take a person's answer only**: a record whose `**스텝**` is `시각-판정`, `금지-후보` or `발행-실패`, or begins with one of them followed by ` — ` (§4), is answered with `답한 주체=사람` alone, and the seat's and a shift's answers to it are refused (exit 3) before any other check — whether the published screens are right, whether a candidate joins the forbidden list and whether a failed publish is given up are judged by looking at the screens, which no recommendation has done. **The seat names it on every call** — a seat call without it is exit 2 — and a row written before the field existed reads `사람`. **A seat run is the seat's too**: `답한 주체=좌석` with `선택지=좌석 실행` answers a record whose `**스텝**` begins with `Step 3 잔여 검증 좌석 실행` or `Step 3 게이트 도달 좌석 실행` and that carries a `` - `좌석 실행` `` option line; the gate takes it whatever `CC_CMDS_AUTOPILOT_AUTO_RESOLVE` says, skips the recommendation checks below, requires `첨부` for the `게이트 도달` form, refuses one whose `**좌석 실행 명령**` line names a token the enforcement-surface classifier classes (`es_class_command`: each whitespace-split token, unquoted and after any `=`, as written and with `/` prefixed), stamps `근거 종류=좌석 실행`, and refuses (exit 3) `선택지=좌석 실행` from the seat on any other record; the latest-settled-halt and one-answer checks below still hold. **A seat apply is a person's answer first, and then the segment's**: `선택지=좌석 적용` and `사람 몫으로 넘김` answer only a record whose `**스텝**` begins with `Step 3 강제 표면 좌석 적용` (§4), carries no `**추천**` line and offers the label as an option line inside its option block, and a shift's call is refused. A person's `좌석 적용` needs a proven question frame: the caller's transcript lineage holds an answered `AskUserQuestion` frame whose question contains, verbatim, the question line `seat-apply-preview` printed for the record (which opens with the record's sha256), whose shown labels are the record's labels as a set, whose answer is the chosen label itself rather than a free answer, and whose id no earlier row cites in `승인 프레임`. A person's `사람 몫으로 넘김` needs no frame and commits nothing. The seat's `좌석 적용` (`답한 주체=좌석`) is the inherited answer: the gate takes it whatever `CC_CMDS_AUTOPILOT_AUTO_RESOLVE` says and skips the recommendation checks below, and only when the record has no `**사람 몫 파일**` and an earlier person's `좌석 적용` row of the same segment in this ledger — not one carried from another run — has its `좌석 커밋` still an ancestor of the segment HEAD, a scope (re-derived and compared with the row's `적용 범위 다이제스트`) holding every path the new record applies, and no later `park`, abandon or adoption row of the segment; the latest-settled-halt, waiting-answer and one-answer checks below still hold. Either `좌석 적용` commits the record's files on `**좌석 적용 기준**` before the row is written, and the branch is reset to the base when the row cannot be. **The seat may answer without a person** with `답한 주체=좌석`, and then the gate decides whether it may: `CC_CMDS_AUTOPILOT_AUTO_RESOLVE` is on (`gate_auto_resolve_enabled`, else exit 3); the caller is the seat, not a shift or a stage; the record is a complete record under this run's `halt/`, `**분류**: gate-unanswerable`, with at least one option line, exactly one `**추천**` line at the start of a line naming one option's label byte for byte (`(없음)` is no label), and a `**스텝**` that does not begin with `Step 3 잔여 검증 좌석 실행` or `Step 3 게이트 도달 좌석 실행` (§4); `선택지` is left out — the gate copies the `**추천**` label — or equals it; the recommended option's description carries none of `위험 수용`, `게이트 비활성`, `그대로 진행`, `범위 축소`, and its whole option line names no token the enforcement-surface classifier classes, since the seat may run a command that option describes; `첨부` is given. The gate stamps `근거 종류`, the `/`-joined kinds of the record's `**추천 근거**` (`미상` when it names none of the four), on that row only. **A seat answer to a design stage's `/ ladder-unsettled` or `/ skeleton` record passes five checks more**, each exit 3, recognised by those substrings of `**스텝**` and never applied to a person's answer: (G1) a `/ ladder-unsettled` record carries exactly one `**잔여 판정**` line in the grammar and vocabulary of §4, no label twice; (G2) its recommended label is exactly `잔여로 두고 동결`, every value is `코드-부재`, `사람-환경` or `외부-준비`, its `**스킬**` is not `design-base-unattended` and its `**스텝**` does not name `설계-골격`; (G3) against the manifest's `설계 문서`, every label is an R-item at `구현 시 검증`, every item at `구현 시 검증` without a W3 line is in the record, each value equals the document's `차단 부류` reading (`미분류` for a missing, out-of-vocabulary or mispaired one), and every `사람-환경` and `외부-준비` item carries the anchored line `검증 시점: 구현 후` — a document the gate cannot read is a refusal; (G4) when the document declares two or more implement slices, each label is in exactly one slice's `**잔여 항목**`; (G5) on both kinds of record, every ` / ` part of `**추천 근거**` has one of the four kinds before its first `:`, and on a `/ ladder-unsettled` record every part is `기기 사실`. On an accepted ladder answer the gate stamps `잔여 판정=<부류>:<R 목록>/<부류>:<R 목록>` (`코드-부재:R2,R3/사람-환경:R1`) on the row; that field is counted in the row's projected size and never clipped, and a row over 1024 bytes once `근거` is clipped to its floor is refused. **Every automatic answer — `교대` or `좌석` — passes five more checks.** **A shift's record under `halt/shift/` (§4) is judged by its own row instead of a `stage-result` row**: it is the gate's only when a cone `blocked` row of this ledger names it in `중단 기록` with a `기록 다이제스트`, and an answer to it — a person's included — is refused (exit 3) unless the file still hashes to that digest, the row is anchored on `--segment`, and `halt/shift` is a real directory at its own place; `--segment` then needs the key's segment row rather than a `stage-result` row, and a file under `halt/shift/` no such row names is exit 2. An automatic answer to it is refused while the key's dispatch record or a waiting member stands, as below, or when a later `blocked` row is anchored on the key or a later `stage-result` row of the key follows the record's row. A stage's record is judged as follows. It goes only to the key's latest settled halt: it is refused while the key's dispatch record `<key>.pid` (or a `.settling` rename of it) is still in the run directory or the key is a waiting member, because that attempt's `stage-result` row would take the answer up with no stage receiving it, and refused unless the key's last `stage-result` row — passing over rows ending `크래시`, `한도 종료` or `외부 종료` — reads `종단 부류=의도된 park` and the record is that attempt's `halt/<스테이지>#<실행 버전>.md` or names that stage id in its header. It is refused while any answer no stage has taken up yet — a person's or an automatic one — names the same record, or while one names another record of the same key: the snapshot lists one answer per key, so an automatic row written over a waiting answer would hand on the recommendation and drop that answer. A `재수렴` answer to a record whose earlier `재수렴` answer (anyone's) was taken up by a `종류=reconverge` row not ending `크래시`, `한도 종료` or `외부 종료` is refused with 「같은 정지 기록을 두 번 재수렴하려면 사람의 답이 필요합니다」. A record holds one automatic answer: a second one is refused while the first is unspent, and after a stage took the first up unless that stage ended `크래시`, `한도 종료` or `외부 종료`. And a `재수렴` answer is counted against the `구속-이탈` row's `상한` over the key's automatic `재수렴` answers — rows reading `답한 주체=교대` or `좌석`, or, written before the field, the `근거` prefix — `없음` unbounded, an integer N admitting N; a person's answer never counts, and with no such row the seat's answers are counted for the report and bounded by nothing else. An automatic `adopt as a requirement` to a `design-audit-unattended` record is refused when the key already has a `종류=reconverge` row not ending `크래시`, `한도 종료` or `외부 종료`, and an automatic answer to a record the shift handed back (below) is refused. A person's answer passes none of these: a person may answer the same record again, and a person's second `재수렴` to the same record or second adoption on the same audit key is routed like the first. **Every writer's answer, a person's included, must be one this run can carry out.** The gate judges the record, the option and the ledger as they stand with one function and one route table — `재부착`, `감사-재수렴`, `골격급-재수렴`, `구속-재수렴`, `교대-정지`, `빈-대상`, `적용-보류` (an answer `보류` to a `Step 3 강제 표면 좌석 적용` record, which the shift carries out by parking the segment), `설계-재수렴` — whose structural conditions (a session id to re-attach, an audit key the gate would key a `- reconverge` on — for `골격급-재수렴`, a `design-audit-unattended` record whose `**스텝**` begins with `Step 7 / 골격급 재발` answered `재수렴`, the same key test as `감사-재수렴` — a segment not yet terminal, a segment row for a shift's record, a cycle budget for `구현 재파견`, a design step whose last row is the `정상 완료` freeze for a `골격 재검토` on its `/ ladder-unsettled` record) apply to every writer; when none holds it writes nothing, exits 3, and prints `라우팅 불가 — <reason>` and `실행할 수 있는 선택지: <labels>` (or `없음`) on stderr. Argv carries `중단 기록` (the record's absolute path, which has to sit under this run's `halt/` directory, open with the record header and close with its fence), `선택지` (one of that record's options, byte for byte — a free answer is not one), `근거` (clipped) and, optionally, `첨부` — a non-empty `.md` file the seat wrote under the same `halt/` with what the person supplied for that option, which must not open with the halt record header; `--segment` names the stage that halted — the segment id, or the step id for the design step — and has to have a `stage-result` row. The gate stamps `세그먼트`, `스킬` (the record's own line), `기록 다이제스트` (the record's `sha256`), `첨부 다이제스트` when there is an attachment, `레인` and `기록 시각`; any other argv key is exit 2 with no row. **It decides nothing**: no termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector. Its one reader is the snapshot's `answered_halts[]`, which lists the last row per key until a `stage-result` row of that key lands on a later line — the stage the shift dispatched with the answer has then taken it — so **each row is spent once**; an answer to a shift's record is spent as well by the first later `segment` row of the key, act a shift takes on the key (an approval row with `결정=act`, `행위자=교대` and the key's `세그먼트`), or `blocked` row anchored on the key with `원인=해소`, since the next shift carries that answer out itself; an answer `세그먼트 park` to a stage's record, and an answer `보류` to a `Step 3 강제 표면 좌석 적용` record, is spent as well by the first later `segment` row of the key reading `상태=park`, since the park that carries it out dispatches nothing; and any answer is spent by a later `스코프=cone 원인=막힘` `blocked` row the gate stamped `막힌 답 줄=<its line>` — the shift's row handing the record back to a person because it could not carry the answer out — in this ledger and in an earlier run's ledger an adoption reads alike. Such a record is **handed back** until a later `중단 답` row names it: every reader of unanswered records counts it as waiting, and the snapshot lists it in `returned_halts[]`. Each `answered_halts[]` element carries `route` and `route_refusal`, the same judgment the answer met when it was written, and `carry_prompt`, the `-p` text of the carry act for an implement, design or split halt the re-entry table holds — the ordinary dispatch form the gate builds from the ledger the attempt halted in, the earlier run's for an answer brought in — and empty for any other answer; each element's `answered_by` is the row's `답한 주체`, read on a row written before the field as `교대` when its `근거` carries the shift's prefix and `사람` otherwise. The automatic-answer checks above read the ledger's `중단 답` rows too, and the morning report lists every automatic one. **An adopted segment's planning act is the gate's own second writer**: it copies an earlier run's open seat answer `재수렴` to a CFI-U3 BT-STOP record, or any earlier answer an `이월` row of that run's ledger names by its `중단 답 줄`, into this run, as described with `세그먼트 입양` above, writing `중단 기록` as the copy under this run's `halt/`, the earlier row's `스킬`, `선택지`, `답한 주체`, `근거 종류` when it has one, `기록 다이제스트`, attachment fields, `레인` and `기록 시각`, `근거` prefixed with `입양 이월(출처 런 <run id> 원장 <line> 번째 줄) — `, and two fields of its own, `출처 런` (the earlier run) and `출처 기록` (the earlier record's path). `answered_halts[]` lists it like any other row, with `origin_run` set from `출처 런`, and spends it the same way. **A re-kickoff's follow-up run brings one in the same way** — the manifest's `사전 인가` row whose first field is `이어받은 런=<run id>` names the earlier run: the first dispatch of a run-scope step (the design, the audit, the split, the visual stage or the visual publish stage) that holds no `stage-result` row yet copies the answer an `이월` row of the earlier run's ledger names on the last same-kind step of that run, to `halt/<step id>#chained-<run id>.md`, writes the row with `근거` prefixed `연쇄 이월(출처 런 <run id> 원장 <line> 번째 줄) — ` and `연쇄 종류` set to the step's kind, and refuses that dispatch with exit 3. An attachment is copied beside the record copy as `<record copy without .md>.answer.md`, the name every count of unanswered records passes over. **An answer carried over twice** — the earlier row is itself such a copy, deferred again before any stage took it up — keeps the earlier row's `출처 런` and `출처 기록`, the run the person answered in, and names the copy it came through as `경유 기록`, while its `근거` prefix still names the run it came through and the line of that run's ledger it was copied from; an earlier run that holds no `stage-result` row of the kind is read for its key by the last `중단 답` row carrying that `연쇄 종류`, and an answer a ledger already names as `출처 기록` or `경유 기록` is not brought in again. **While an answer brought in under an `이월` row is unspent, every dispatch of its key but a carry act (`--carry-answer`) is refused with exit 3**, and the carry act judges the record's currency and a re-convergence's scope against the earlier run's ledger.

**The ladder record's `**잔여 판정**` line and the scope string that undoes a ladder freeze are defined here once.** The line sits after `**추천 근거**` and before `**하네스 오류**` of a `/ ladder-unsettled` record (§4) and reads `**잔여 판정**: <부류>: R<n>, R<n> / <부류>: R<n>` — `<부류>` one of `코드-부재` · `사람-환경` · `외부-준비` · `전제-반증` · `미분류`, each `R<n>` the number of a `### R<n>.` heading of the design document, listed once. `전제-반증` marks an item whose premise the ladder's first rung refuted; the other four are the document's `차단 부류` reading (`_common/verification.md` §5). Such a record recommends `` `잔여로 두고 동결` `` only when every value is one of the first three, every `사람-환경` and `외부-준비` item carries the whole line `**검증 시점**: 구현 후`, and the writer is not `design-base-unattended`, whose ladder record always writes `(없음)`; its `**추천 근거**` is one `기기 사실:<문서> 의 R<n> 차단 부류=<값>` part per item. **`잔여 동결 재검토`** is the scope string the routing shift, `design-reconverge` and the gate quote byte for byte: `/cc-cmds:design-reconverge <document> 잔여 동결 재검토 <halt record absolute path>` re-opens the R-items a `/ ladder-unsettled` record listed and the skeleton they rest on, after a person answered it `골격 재검토`. That person's answer comes back as this re-convergence, not as a re-attachment, once the document is frozen — the gate routes it `설계-재수렴`; one that arrives before the freeze is routed `재부착` and re-attaches the session, which writes its `**잔여 동결**` line and freezes, and the same re-convergence follows.

**A person's answer to a halted re-convergence is settled without a session when its outcome is already fixed.** `design-reconverge` writes an option in the machine settlement grammar only when its edit is on disk and its verdict is computed (its `Halt record` rule); the option line then reads, as a whole line,

```
- `반영 확인` — 검사 sha256 /Users/me/repo/docs/feature.md=0f1e2d3c4b5a69788796a5b4c3d2e1f00f1e2d3c4b5a69788796a5b4c3d2e1f0 → 종단 재설계 필요
```

— the label, `검사 sha256 `, the design document's absolute path, `=`, its 64-hex `sha256`, ` → 종단 `, and `재설계 필요` or `불필요`, nothing after it. Before carrying an answer whose record carries such a line, the routing shift calls `act --kind halt-settle --segment <key> -- '중단 답 줄=<n>'`, a bookkeeping kind graded `읽기` that a stage may not call (exit 3); argv carries that one key (any other is exit 2 with no row), and `--segment` is the answer row's `세그먼트` — the step id for a run-scope step — so `-` is exit 2 with no row as for every row act. The gate settles only when every check holds, and otherwise writes nothing and exits 3, leaving the answer open for the carry act: line `<n>` is a `중단 답` row of `--segment` with `스킬=design-reconverge` and no `출처 런`; the answered option's line in the record parses in the grammar above; its path is the manifest's design document and that document's `sha256` equals the line's value now; the record still hashes to the row's `기록 다이제스트`; the record is the key's last `의도된 park` attempt's; its `**관측 상세**` carries `범위=감사 종합 요구 <path>` or `범위=구속 티어 이탈 <path>` that the carry act's scope check accepts — `R<n>` and every other scope are refused, because a carrier block only a session closes would outlive the run; no dispatch record, live stage or waiting member of the key stands; the answer is not spent, no later `중단 답` of the key follows it, and no `이월` row names it. It then claims the key's next `실행 버전` the way a routed dispatch does and writes one `stage-result` row — `종류=reconverge`, `종료 코드=-`, `세션 id=-`, `부모=-`, `기록자=게이트`, `effort=(미상)`, `서빙 모델=(미상)`, `재수렴 판정` the line's verdict, `종단 부류=정상 완료`, `정산=중단 답 줄 <n>`, `관측=중단 답 정산` — and voids the segment's plan as a re-convergence row from a stream does. That row spends the answer, so a second call is refused; a sibling's `replan` cites it by `<스테이지>#<실행 버전>` like any re-convergence row. It launches nothing, asks no router and writes in no tree, so a run past its deadline takes it as well.

**The twenty-eighth exists because a person's dismissal of review findings has to reach the merge checks, which read only the segment's last `cycle` row.** `리뷰 기각` is written by `act --kind review-dismiss`, by the seat only, on the same terms as `한도 해제`. Argv carries `사이클` and `리뷰 HEAD`, which must equal the segment's last `cycle` row — a person dismisses the review they looked at — and `근거` (clipped); a cycle with P0 and P1 both zero has nothing to dismiss, and any other argv key is exit 2 with no row. The gate stamps `세그먼트`, `기각 P0`·`기각 P1` and `리포트 경로` from that cycle row, `레인` and `기록 시각`. It dismisses the whole cycle, because the `cycle` row counts findings rather than naming them. **It binds by value, not by position**: a reader honours it only while the segment's last `cycle` row carries the same `사이클` and `리뷰 HEAD`, so a newer review voids it without anything being rewritten. Its readers are the `리뷰-후-머지` rule (through the gate's `GATE_SEGMENT_REVIEW_DISMISSED`), the apply act's precondition 3, the snapshot's `cycles[].기각`, and the merge verdict's count half, which reads it before the counts and records `머지 대조=기각` on a `머지됨` row whose review covers the merged tip; the freshness ladder and the report check stand unchanged. No termination condition reads the row itself — condition 11 reads the `머지 대조` it leads to — and it is outside the progress vector.

**The twenty-ninth exists because the open P1 findings of a review cycle can be carried over to issues instead of fixed before the merge, and the merge checks read only the segment's last `cycle` row.** `리뷰 이월` is written by `act --kind review-carry` — and by the driver, through the same gate function `gate_review_carry_row`, so the checks and the row cannot differ between the two paths. **It is not seat only**, unlike `리뷰 기각`: a dismissal is a person overriding a review, while a carry-over is the routing shift and the driver doing what the review itself classified, and the eligibility check is what holds them to that classification. Argv carries `사이클` and `리뷰 HEAD`, which must equal the segment's last `cycle` row, `건수`, which must equal that row's `P1` — every carry-over item is carried — and `이슈`, exactly `건수` distinct issue URLs of the target's repository (`https://github.com/<slug>/issues/<n>`), comma-separated, in the order the report's carry-over items appear; any other argv key, a missing one, a cycle or head that is not the last row's, a count that is not its `P1`, and issues of another repository, duplicated or miscounted, are exit 2 with no row. **Each URL is asked of the repository, not only read as a shape**: the k-th must be an open issue — not a pull request — whose title starts `[이월] <run id> <segment> 사이클 ` and ends ` — <the k-th 이월 제목 of the report>`, so a URL that names some other issue is exit 2 with no row, and a repository the gate cannot ask (no `gh`, no answer) is exit 3 with no row. A row that does not fit the cap is exit 2 too: an issue URL is never clipped, because a clipped URL names another issue or none. The gate stamps `세그먼트` from `--segment`, `레인` and `기록 시각`. **The last cycle has to be eligible**, or the act is exit 3 with no row: it is the re-check — a lead-fix row of the segment precedes it — so a cycle is carried over only after the lead fix; P0 is zero; the report's `P1 분류` line parses; it counts no `차단` item; its `이월 가능` count equals the report's summary P1 and the row's P1, at least 1 and with no upper bound — every P1 is carried or none is; the P1 section's tag lines keep the grammar and their counts equal the `P1 분류` line; every carry-over item carries a title and a body with no `docs/` in it; and the `리뷰 이월` row, with an issue URL of the longest form per item, fits the row cap, since a URL is never clipped. The conditions are numbered 1 to 7 and 9 in the gate; 8, the manifest preauthorizing `gh issue`, is gone — the routing shift opens the issues under its declared reach — and the number stays free. On a re-check a failed condition reads the ladder's next step — `리드 수정` or `재수렴` — while K is within `RECHECK_ROUND_CAP`, and `다음=잔여 보류` past it. **One row per cycle**: a second row for the same `<사이클>@<리뷰 HEAD>` is exit 3, so a shift that retries after its row landed reads `다음=이월 머지` and goes to the merge. **It binds by value, not by position**, the binding a dismissal takes: a reader honours it only while the segment's last `cycle` row carries the same `사이클` and `리뷰 HEAD`. Its readers are the `리뷰-후-머지` rule (through the gate's `GATE_SEGMENT_REVIEW_CARRIED`), which passes a cycle with P0 zero and P1 above zero only when it was carried over or dismissed, the apply act's precondition 3, which takes a carried-over cycle beside a dismissed one so a segment merged after a carry-over is not stopped at its apply, the snapshot's `review_next[].다음`, which reads `이월 머지` for such a cycle, and the merge verdict's count half, which reads `이월` — that half reads a dismissal of the same cycle too, before the counts as the `다음` table does, and then reads `기각`, P0 included. The `리뷰-후-적용` rule does not read it. It is a bookkeeping kind, no termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector: the merge that follows moves the segment's `상태`, and a carry-over without a merge is not progress. The order the issues and the row are written in is `_common/carryover-issue.md`.

**The thirtieth exists because the design tier the run was planned on has to be read in the morning beside the reviews, and the kickoff that judged it writes no ledger.** `설계 티어` is written by **the gate's run-open branch only** (`gate_record_design_tier`), immediately after the `run` row: run open happens once per run and before the driver's first stage, so this one writer covers the routed path and the fixed-graph driver alike, and **the driver and `feed.sh` neither write nor relay it**. It is written when the manifest's plan carries `design_tier_judged` and the ledger holds no `설계 티어` row yet; a plan frozen before the key existed gets no row, and a second entry writes none. `판정` is the kickoff's `design_tier_judged` and `확정` the frozen `design_tier`, so **a person's raise reads as `판정 ≠ 확정`** on this row and nowhere else. `범위` is the plan's design scope, `경로` who writes the design — `base` on a base scope, otherwise the plan's `design_path` (`solo-audit` \| `discuss-audit`) with `none` written `해당 없음`, a plan without the key read by the absence rule of the contract sentence on `manifest_design_skill` — `설계 스킬` the skill the selection function picks for the run's design step (`없음` with no design step), `감사` whether the plan has a `design-audit` step (`있음` \| `없음`), and `추정 파일` the plan's `estimated_files`. **`사유` and `근거` are clipped excerpts** — the heavy reasons joined by ` / ` (200 bytes) and `design_tier_rationale` (240 bytes), each `없음` when absent — and their originals stay in the manifest's plan. It is not `자율 승인`: that series records what the run decided, and the tier was decided before the run. **No termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector** — `gate_progress_vector` selects series by name, so the number of lines to change for that is zero.

**The thirty-ninth exists because whether a lead-only design followed by an audit is as good as a discussion followed by one can only be judged from the audits' own counts, compared within one audit edition.** `감사 공시` is one row per audit attempt. **Two writers, one builder** (`gate_record_audit_disclosure`): the gate's audit recorder writes it right after an audit's `stage-result` row, and the fixed-graph driver writes it through `run_gate_call` after its `S2` row and after a continued `S2` attempt's row; a re-audit is dispatched through the same place and gets its row there. The reconcile path writes none. `세그먼트` is `-`, `스테이지` the audit step and `실행 버전` the attempt. `경로` takes the `설계 티어` row's words, and `미상` only when the plan block cannot be read. `회차` is `재감사` when the key carries a re-convergence in this run (`audit_pass_of`) and `최초` otherwise. `감사 판` is the first 12 hex of the sha256 over the audit's instruction files read from the run's plugin copy — `design-audit-unattended/SKILL.md`, `design-audit/references/01`..`04`, then the `audit-reader` row of `_common/team-model-tier.md` chosen by its first cell — and `미상` on a run with no `plugin-pin`; `플러그인` is the pin's `<version>@<commit 12>`. `원시`, `고유`, `미보강` and `바이트` are read between the disclosure v1 fence pair only, and `골격급`, `합성 후보` and `합성 골격급` between the skeletal fence pair only; a report without exactly one pair, a missing slot and a value that is not a count read `미상`. Whether a re-convergence fired and what it concluded is read from the key's `종류=reconverge` rows, not repeated here. **No termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector**; its readers are `collect-run-metrics.sh`'s `design_paths`, `design-path-report.sh` and the morning report's one line per row. There is no trigger: nothing switches or reverts a path from it.

**`중단 리포트 건너뜀` is the fortieth because a stop that was not reported has to be told apart from a run that had nothing to report.** The gate's halt-report round — a detached child started from the prelude, like the metrics round — looks at every settled run on the host that has a record under `<state root>/cc-cmds/halt-report/runs/`, derives its stop events with `run-stops.sh`, and files each unintended signature as a GitHub issue. When it does not file one — the event is one of the four intended waits and was still open when the run settled, there is no write-scoped credential, the credential lacks the `repo`·`project` scopes, or the identity or open-issue lookup failed — the event would otherwise vanish, and `계측 필링 건너뜀` cannot carry it: that series' `사유` vocabulary and its `트리거` belong to the metrics collector. The filings that DID happen go on `자율 승인` rows with `kind=halt-report` and `절단점=필링`. Both rows land in the ledger of the run whose gate HOSTED the round, so they usually describe another run: `런` names it, and the run's own record carries its disposition. Both carry `세그먼트=-` and **no `행위자` and no `판단 부류` field**, for the reason the metrics rows carry none. `자격 없음` and `스코프 부족` are final for that run, so putting a credential in later does not file a backlog of old runs at once; `조회 실패` is looked at again for three rounds. A Project add that failed is retried on later rounds without a new row — only the record's `담기` changes. **It decides nothing**: no termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector.

**The forty-first exists because what a design team's late passes did — whether a third round opened and why, what the fidelity and coherence passes found — leaves no trace once the team is gone except the witness corpus, and the corpus is read by no one.** `토론 패스` is one row per design attempt that ran a team. **Two writers, one builder** (`gate_record_discussion_passes`): the gate's stage-result recorder writes it right after a `종류=design` row, and the fixed-graph driver writes it through `run_gate_call` after its `S1design` row and after a continued `S1design` attempt's row; those two rows carry no `종류`, so the driver places it by the stage id. The builder reads `$RUN_DIR/design/<slug>/witness/` and writes no row when the directory is absent or holds no `*.round-1.md` — the solo branch has no team. **One witness per member and round or phase**: of `{role-slug}.<P>.md` and its revisions `{role-slug}.<P>-rev<k>.md` the one with the largest k (compared as a number) is chosen, the original only when no revision exists, and the two are never counted together; a name outside that grammar (`INDEX.md`, `SWEEP.md`, `*.prompt.md`) is not a witness. A round-2 witness's declaration is its last line that is exactly `round-3: none` or starts `round-3: measure — `, chosen across both forms. `세그먼트` is `-`, `스테이지` the design step and `실행 버전` the attempt; `경로` takes the `설계 티어` row's words; `팀원` is the number of round-1 witnesses; `R3` is `열림` when any round-3 witness exists and `닫힘` otherwise; `측정 지명` is the number of round-2 witnesses declaring `measure`, `미상` when that is zero and some round-2 witness declares nothing; `스윕 반박` is the number of lines of `SWEEP.md` starting `round-3: sweep — `, `미상` when the file is absent. **`R3 사유` is the first of five lines that holds**: closed with `측정 지명` ≥ 1 `지명 미개방`; any other closed `해당 없음`; open with `측정 지명` ≥ 1 `측정 지명`; any round-2 witness without a declaration `미상`; what is left `선언 없음`. Sweep rebuttals are no entry to round 3 and stay out of the rule. `R3 분` is the minutes, rounded down, from the newest round-2 witness's modification time to the newest round-3 witness's, `미상` when round 3 is closed or a time cannot be read. `충실도 좌석` and `정합 좌석` count the chosen `fidelity` and `coherence` witnesses; `충실도 발견` and `정합 발견` sum each one's last line that is exactly `findings: <n>`, `미상` with no seat or when any seat lacks the line; `충실도 재개` counts `- type: decision-reopen` lines in the fidelity witnesses, `미상` with no seat. **Each (run id, design step) is counted by its last row in ledger order**, so a continued attempt replaces the earlier one. **No termination condition reads it and it is outside the progress vector**; its readers are `collect-run-metrics.sh`'s `discussion_passes` and its verdict block and `design-path-report.sh`, and the morning report does not list it. There is no trigger: nothing opens or closes a round from it.

**The thirty-first exists because a reviewed segment that conflicts with its base has to be told apart from one that merges, and `blocked` would hold condition 5 on the two verdicts that are not blocks.** `베이스 판정` is written by `act --kind base-check` only, which takes nothing after `--`: every field is the gate's. The gate refuses with exit 3 and no row unless the segment's review lets a merge through — `다음` of the review table is `머지` or `이월 머지`, and R is the last cycle's `리뷰 HEAD` — and when the worktree HEAD has moved past R into anything but R's tree or the renumbering merge described below (`다음` is then `해소 리뷰`). The base T is what `git ls-remote https://github.com/<원격 슬러그>.git refs/heads/<베이스 브랜치>` answers, with `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`; a local tracking ref is never read, because a spelling the grading table reads as a read moves it. The gate does not fetch: a T the repository does not hold yet is exit 3 naming the fetch. The merge is computed with `git merge-tree --write-tree` in an object directory of its own that reads the repository through `GIT_ALTERNATE_OBJECT_DIRECTORIES` and is removed afterwards, so a check leaves no object behind. A second check of the same R, HEAD and T writes nothing — the conflict cap counts code-conflict rows, `충돌` rows whose `충돌 파일` names a path outside the two release files (a cell without the key, empty, `-` or clipped counts), so a repeated row would spend it on nothing. `판정` is `일치` (T is an ancestor of the judged HEAD), `이동` (T moved and the merge is clean) or `충돌`; `부류` is `릴리스 메타` when three things hold — the judged HEAD is R or the head of a renumbering chain of R the gate proves (a HEAD that only has R's tree is not), the judged HEAD's merge with T conflicts in at least one path and in the two release files alone, and every premise of the expected release tree of R and T holds — `일반` for any other conflict, and `-` when there is none. A `릴리스 메타` row is therefore never a code-conflict row.

**The expected release tree is what lets a release-metadata conflict skip a review.** Two releases of the same plugin conflict in `plugins/cc-cmds/.claude-plugin/plugin.json` and `CHANGELOG.md` and nowhere else, and the resolution is not a judgment: the segment's version is the base's bumped by the grade the segment bumped its own, and its changelog entry goes on top of the base's. With MB the merge base of R and T, the premises are: the merge conflicts in those two files at most; from MB to R `plugin.json` changes in its one version line only, compared by lines; R's changelog is MB's with one entry added on top and the preamble unchanged; MB's version to R's is one step with no suffix, and R's head entry names R's version; T's `plugin.json` version is T's head entry's; and R's head entry body carries no `X.Y.Z` token. The tree E\* is the clean merge with `plugin.json` as T's bytes with the version line bumped from T's by R's grade, and the changelog as T's preamble, R's head entry under the new number and R's date, and T's entries. A merge commit H is **the renumbering merge** of R — the head of a renumbering chain — when, walking down first parents from H, every link L holds: L has exactly two parents p1 and p2; p2 is T or an ancestor of it for H, and for a lower link a proper ancestor (an ancestor and not the same commit) of the p2 of the link above; L's tree is E\*(R, p2) to the object id; and p1 is R, or p1 is the next link. A chain of one link is a merge whose parents are exactly R and some X, X is T or an ancestor of it, with tree E\*(R, X). The tree is compared whole — never a path list or a conflict list, because two same-grade bumps merge `plugin.json` cleanly to the wrong number and a path list hides a change inside a file — and every link's tree is fixed by R and its base alone, so a chain lets no tree through that a single renumbering merge would not. `gate.sh meta-merge --manifest <m> --segment <id>` builds the next link in the segment worktree from the latest `베이스 판정` row when it reads `충돌` with `부류=릴리스 메타`: its first parent is the worktree HEAD, its second parent that row's base T and its tree E\*(R, T). It refuses with exit 3 when that row's `판정 HEAD` is not the worktree HEAD, when the HEAD is neither R nor a renumbering chain head of R proved onto T, and when T is already an ancestor of the HEAD, so one base tip is never resolved twice; otherwise it fast-forwards the branch to the commit and prints its sha. It pushes nothing and writes no row.

**The thirty-second exists because a run a reboot cut has to be called again by something that is not a shift, and the morning has to tell that call apart from a shift's own launch.** `재진입` is written by `act --kind reentry` only, and only by a boot-time re-entry — `fleet.sh`'s dispatch tick, which carries `CC_PIPELINE_REENTRY=<run id>@<boot epoch>` and no other pipeline marker; a caller with its own shift ordinal, a stage, or a caller carrying `CC_PIPELINE_SHIFT_ID` is refused with exit 3 and no row. Argv carries `사유` — the reason the re-entry is about to issue, `대기`, `중단` or `상한` — and `레이블`, the dispatch label that ran it; any other key, `대상`, `부팅 시각` and `기록 시각` included, is exit 2 with no row, because the target, the boot the kernel reports and the time are the gate's. The row is written in the same tick as, and before, the `router-shift` it precedes, so its `부팅 시각` against its `기록 시각` is the time from boot to the first tick after login. **Its one reader is the re-attachment base**: a `재진입` row below a cut stage's `stage-result` row makes that row's time the base of the stage's next re-attachment (`<stage>.anchor`, §6), taken over a limit-class base when it is the later of the two. It is a bookkeeping kind: no termination condition reads it, it is outside the progress vector, and nothing is notified from it.

**The thirty-fourth to thirty-seventh exist because a target can carry its own PreToolUse Bash hook, and what that hook refuses happens after the run's hook allowed the call — outside the ledger unless the gate writes it down.** None of the four is written from argv; every field is the gate's.

- **`대상 훅`** records the Bash hooks of an issuing root: the gate writes it the first time it sees the root and whenever the root's hook digest changes, so a person editing a guard leaves a row and moves H. The digest covers the guard files the hook commands name, not only the settings lines.
- **`대상 거절`** records one refusal by the target's hook. `형태=감쌈` is a gate call the run hook found the root's hook refusing when it ran that hook first on the exact bytes: the run hook writes the record under `admit/refused/` and the gate turns it into a row on its next call (`근거=감쌈 예측`; `행위자` from the record's caller kind). `형태=맨` with `근거=예측` is a bare command that `admit` ran through the root's hook and found refused; with `근거=추정` it is an issued write whose ticket was spent more than thirty seconds ago and whose effect a later remote read did not show. `조건` is the sha256 of five elements — the root's hook digest, the segment worktree's HEAD, the remote tip and the PR from the last `원격 판독` row, and the segment's last `중단 답` row — and **an act whose digest and form match a row whose condition is still equal is refused with exit 3 and no new row**, because the same act under the same condition meets the same refusal; any one element moving lets it through again. One row is written per act, form and condition.
- **`원격 판독`** records the segment branch's remote tip and its pull request as `git ls-remote` and `gh pr list` answer them, beside the worktree HEAD, and only when one of them changed. A read that fails writes `미상` and refuses nothing. It is written by `act --kind remote-check`, which takes nothing after `--` and is a bookkeeping kind, by an `admit` of a push, PR or merge rung, and for every non-terminal segment of the target before a shift launches. `gate_snapshot` never reads the remote.
- **`맨 명령 결과`** closes an admitted bare command: `판정=관측` when the remote read after the ticket was spent shows the write took effect, `판정=미상` for an admitted read, whose effect cannot be seen.

**`admit` is the second permitted shape of a remote act in a hooked target.** The run hook refuses a bare command and records its bytes as a request under `admit/req/<caller key>`; `gate.sh admit --request <id|last> --manifest … --target … [--segment …] --surface … [--reach …] --snapshot-digest … --rationale …` takes no argv, splits the recorded bytes with a closed grammar (exit 2 outside it), grades them like `exec` — the cutpoint derived from the words when none is declared, raised to `push` for a push — and writes the `자율 승인` row with `형태=맨`, `요청`, `티켓` and `예측`. It refuses (exit 3) a request older than three hundred seconds, one of another caller, a push whose refspec is `HEAD` outside the segment worktree, an issuing root that is not the target's own checkout while the target's main worktree carries a Bash hook, and a write under a separated credential, which a bare command cannot carry. Then it runs the root's hooks on the bytes: a refusal, `ask`, `defer`, the failure of a hook entry carrying `onFailure: "block"`, a hook that outlives the probe's time bound (`시간 초과(<n>초)`) and settings that cannot be read are a `대상 거절` row and exit 3; any other hook failure (an exit other than 0 or 2, a hook that cannot start, JSON output that does not parse) is `비차단 오류`, which the harness runs the tool after, and counts as a pass. A pass issues a one-use ticket under `admit/ticket/` and prints the bytes to issue again, verbatim, within three hundred seconds from the same directory. **`admit` itself runs nothing**; the run hook spends the ticket when the same bytes come back.

**The snapshot carries what a shift needs to push and open a PR without reading logs.** `guard_refusals[]` lists the newest `대상 거절` row per act and form, at most twenty, with `대상`, `세그먼트`, `형태`, `근거`, `행위 다이제스트`, `명령`, `거절 문면` and `조건` — `같음` while the condition is unchanged, `바뀜` once it moved. Every `segments[]` element carries `적용`, right after `상태`: `대기` when the last `segment` row is `머지됨` carrying `적용=대기`, `미정산` when the last row is `머지됨` carrying `적용=미정산` (a merge brought in from an earlier run whose apply never said how it ended), `인계` when the last row is `머지됨`, a frozen apply command exists, its `적용 주체` is `사람` and no `적용 인계` row names the segment yet, and `없음` otherwise, never `null`. The apply act's preconditions read the same value and end an apply on `없음` with exit 0 and no row, and refuse it on `미정산`. Three lists say where an apply stands: `live_applies` (segments whose apply supervisor is alive or was launched within the last minute), `apply_unsettled` (segments whose last row is `적용 준비` with no live supervisor, or `park 적용=불명`, that a settlement may measure now) and `apply_imported` (segments whose last row is `머지됨 적용=미정산` and on which no `들여온 적용 정산` stop stands unanswered or answered and not yet carried out). Every element also carries `브랜치`, `HEAD`, `원격 팁`, `PR` (`번호`, `상태`, `HEAD`) from the last `원격 판독` row, `원격 단계` — `push 필요`, `PR 필요`, `PR 있음`, `머지됨`, `불일치` or `미상`, `미상` when the last read was not taken at the live HEAD — and `리뷰 줄`, the line `review-line` prints for the segment (`null` without a branch).

**A routing shift stops one segment with `act --kind shift-halt`, which writes a halt record and a cone `blocked` row and no row kind of its own.** The caller has to be a routing shift (a stage or the seat is exit 3). Argv carries `--segment` (a segment the ledger has a row for), the site as `--site <자리>` or `자리=<자리>` (one of the gate's closed site table, else exit 2), `질문`, `관측` and, optionally, `추천` with `추천 근거`; any other key is exit 2. The table's sites are `적용 전제 거부`, `병합 거절`, `push원격불일치`, `도달 거부`, `베이스 해소 한도`, `워크트리 기록 없음`, `종료 조건 11 판독 불가`, `대상 훅 거절`, `적용 정산`, `들여온 적용 정산` and `종료 절 근거`; each but the last offers the options `재시도` and `세그먼트 park`, `적용 정산` adds `재실행` and `사람 확인 정산`, `들여온 적용 정산` adds `사람 확인 정산`, and the next shift carries out the one answered. The gate refuses `재실행` and `사람 확인 정산` answers whose `답한 주체` is not `사람`, and a `세그먼트 park` answer at `적용 정산` also releases the segment's run-scope `blocked 사유=적용 불명` row. `종료 절 근거` asks a person for a termination clause's evidence and stops no segment: it takes `--segment -` and `절=<id>` (`절` at any other site, or a segment on this one, is exit 2), and is refused with exit 3 when the manifest declares no such clause, when the clause's last `종료 절` row reads `충족` or `불가능`, or when an earlier request on it still waits for its answer to be carried out. The gate makes the key `clause-<id>`, writes the record as `halt/shift/clause-<id>#<n>.md` with the options `근거 제출` and `불가능으로 정산`, and anchors the cone on that key alone; that cone holds no segment and is not a run block. A `근거 제출` answer carries `첨부=` the `.answer.md` beside the record or is exit 3, and a later `종료 절` row of the same id, or a new request on the same key, takes the answer up. At `대상 훅 거절` the answer is itself one of the five elements of the refusal condition (`대상 거절` below), so a `재시도` answer lets the next shift issue the blocked act once more. At `도달 거부` the answer moves no part of the gate's judgment: when the parked act is `act --kind ci-rerun`, a `재시도` whose `답한 주체` is `사람` is read by the rerun act itself as this head's rerun round — a person reran the failing run on GitHub or took the failure on the screen as its result — and the act records that round and sends no rerun; any other act, and an answer no person gave, parks again on the same judgment and stops the segment at the site again. At `push원격불일치`, `도달 거부`, `베이스 해소 한도`, `대상 훅 거절`, `적용 정산`, `들여온 적용 정산` and `종료 절 근거` the gate drops any recommendation and writes `(없음)`. Elsewhere `추천` has to be one of the two labels and every part of `추천 근거` a `인터뷰 기록` (the run's `<run id>.interview.md` beside the manifest), `매니페스트 인가` (the manifest) or `상시 지침` (the target's `CLAUDE.md`) quote found verbatim in that file; `기기 사실`, a kind the gate does not know, or a quote not found is exit 3 with nothing written. The gate writes the record (§4) into `halt/shift/` — refused with exit 3 and nothing written unless that is a real directory at its own place, and when a file already sits at the name (`mv -n`) — and then the cone row `스코프=cone`, `원인=막힘`, `앵커 세그먼트=<segment>`, `사유=교대 정지 — <자리>`, `근거` (the clipped `관측`), `중단 기록`, `기록 다이제스트` (the `sha256` of the bytes written), `자리` and `교대`. Those four fields ride on no other `blocked` row: `act --kind blocked` carrying any of them is exit 2. The gate does not refuse acts on the cone; the shift holds it. The snapshot's `shift_halts[]` lists every such row whose record no `중단 답` row has answered yet — `세그먼트`, `자리`, `기록`, `줄` and `보류` (the cone's members now, the anchor left out) — together with any cone row whose `사유` opens with `교대 정지 기록 불가 — `, which has no record and holds its segment for the rest of the run; an element whose segment is terminal is left out, except an `적용 정산` stop on a segment whose last row is a `park` carrying `정산 판정` and a `들여온 적용 정산` stop on a segment whose last row is `머지됨 적용=미정산`. A stop whose slice has no `segment` row yet (a refused predecessor landing or adoption) and an apply whose command never ran (`park 적용=사전 실패`) are not sites: the first has no segment to anchor on, and the second is already a terminal `park` whose apply no answer could retry.

**The rules read four values the gate derives, never the ledger.** Before a rule runs the gate exports `GATE_SEGMENT_LAST_CYCLE` (the body of the segment's last `cycle` row, chosen by field equality on `세그먼트`), `GATE_SEGMENT_LAST_SEGMENT` (the body of the segment's last `segment` row, chosen by field equality on `id`), `GATE_SEGMENT_REVIEW_DISMISSED` and `GATE_SEGMENT_REVIEW_CARRIED` (each the `<사이클>@<리뷰 HEAD>` of the last cycle when a `리뷰 기각` or a `리뷰 이월` row binds it, empty otherwise). On a merge act whose worktree HEAD is not R the gate also exports `GATE_META_RESOLVED_HEAD` — that HEAD — when it proves it the renumbering merge of R onto the base the latest `베이스 판정` row named, and clears the variable on entry otherwise, so a caller cannot set it; the merge rule passes a HEAD equal to it. A rule that picked a row by substring would take another segment's `cycle` row whose free text names this one; that is why the selection is the gate's. An empty or non-canonical `P0` or `P1` is never read as zero.

**`review_next[]` is the one value the routers branch on after a review.** The snapshot carries one element per segment that has a `cycle` row and has not merged: `세그먼트`, `다음`, and the last cycle's `사이클`, `리뷰 HEAD` and `리포트 경로` (absolute — what a carry-over act, the lead fix and the re-check name). A segment with no `cycle` row is left out; its `다음` is `리뷰 없음` by definition. `review_carry` carries the constants `REIMPL_CYCLE_CAP` (2) and `RECHECK_ROUND_CAP` (2) for display only — the routers branch on `다음`, never on a constant. A **lead-fix row** is a `stage-result` row of the segment with `종류=lead-fix`, or the driver's `스테이지=S5F` with no `종류`; a **re-convergence row** is one with `종류=reconverge`, `정상` when its `종단 부류` is `정상 완료`; a **re-authoring row** after the last cycle row is a lead-fix row after it, or a `stage-result` row with `종류=implement` (the driver's `스테이지=S4` with no `종류`) after a `정상` re-convergence row that itself follows the last cycle row — a re-implementation. Any other implement row, such as the base resolution after a carried-over re-check, re-authors nothing, so `이월 머지` and the base overlay's `해소 리뷰` above it stand. The **re-check** is every `cycle` row after the segment's first lead-fix row in the ledger — the review after a re-implementation is a re-check too — and **K** is the number of them. `다음` is the first row of this table that holds:

| Order | Condition | `다음` | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | the segment has no `cycle` row | `리뷰 없음` | dispatch a review; do not merge |
| 2 | a `리뷰 기각` row binds the last cycle | `머지` | merge — a person dismissed the cycle |
| 3 | the last cycle's `P0` or `P1` is empty or not canonical | `판정 불가` | park the segment |
| 4 | P0 = 0, P1 = 0 | `머지` | merge |
| 5 | a lead-fix row exists, a re-authoring row follows the last cycle row | `확인 리뷰` | dispatch the review with `--recheck <리포트 경로>` — unless the segment has an `implement_next[]` element whose `다음` is not `리뷰`, which routes it first |
| 6 | a lead-fix row exists, a re-convergence row that is not `정상` follows the last cycle row | `리드 수정` | dispatch the lead fix once |
| 7 | a lead-fix row exists, a `정상` re-convergence row follows the last cycle row | `재구현` | dispatch the implementation with `수정 대상: <리포트 경로>`; the cycle cap does not refuse it, nor the same re-implementation's later runs while `implement_next[]` reads `구현` |
| 8 | re-check, P0 = 0, P1 > 0, a `리뷰 이월` row binds the last cycle | `이월 머지` | merge |
| 9 | re-check, P0 = 0, P1 > 0, the last cycle is eligible | `이월` | issues, then the `리뷰 이월` row, then read `다음` again and merge on `이월 머지` |
| 10 | re-check, K > `RECHECK_ROUND_CAP` | `잔여 보류` | one issue from `gate.sh residual-body`, then a `스코프=cone` park with `사유=리뷰 잔여` and `이슈=<url>` |
| 11 | re-check, the manifest names a design document, the last cycle's report counts at least one `설계` item in its `결함 수준` lines, and no re-convergence row follows the first lead-fix row | `재수렴` | dispatch `/cc-cmds:design-reconverge <문서> "설계 수준 잔여 <리포트 경로>"` once |
| 12 | re-check, otherwise | `리드 수정` | dispatch the lead fix once, in the segment's worktree, with `--lead-fix <리포트 경로>` |
| 13 | `cycle` rows ≥ `REIMPL_CYCLE_CAP`, no lead-fix row | `리드 수정` | as row 12 |
| 14 | otherwise | `수정` | the fix cycle |

**The ladder after the cap ends by its re-check count, never by a park on the way.** Rows 5 to 12 turn a segment that reached the cap round lead fix → re-check, or, once, re-convergence → re-implementation → re-check, until a re-check passes, carries over, or is the `RECHECK_ROUND_CAP + 1`-th — so a segment gets at most three re-checks. A re-convergence that did not end `정상 완료` is not a park: row 6 sends the segment to the lead fix, and row 11 cannot hold for it again. A `결함 수준` line that does not parse reads as no design-level item, so it sends the ladder the cheaper way and never parks; the report grammar is the review report template's `결함 수준` lines.

**On a routed run the base overlay lies on the two merging values.** When the table above reads `머지` or `이월 머지`, R is the last cycle's `리뷰 HEAD` and the gate reads on, in this order; on any other value, and on the fixed-graph driver, `다음` is the table's. A worktree or a sha that does not resolve leaves the table's value — the merge rule reads the same HEAD and refuses on its own.

| Order | Condition | `다음` | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B1 | the worktree HEAD moved past R, is not R's tree, and is not a renumbering merge of R that the gate proves against the latest `베이스 판정` row's `베이스` (the proof recomputes the expected tree; it does not read that row's `부류`, which a check of the merge itself writes as `-`) | `해소 리뷰` | dispatch the review with the three resolution flags from `review_next[]` |
| B2 | the subject — R, R's tree, or that proved renumbering merge — is the latest `베이스 판정` row's `판정 HEAD`, that row reads `충돌`, and the segment has more than three code-conflict rows — `충돌` rows whose `충돌 파일` names a path outside the two release files | `베이스 해소 한도` | `shift-halt` at the site `베이스 해소 한도` |
| B3 | as B2, with three code-conflict rows or fewer | `베이스 해소` | dispatch `implement` whose prompt carries `베이스 해소: <베이스>` |
| B4 | otherwise | the table's | `act --kind base-check` after the fetch, then merge on `일치` or `이동` |

At the three base values `review_next[]` also carries `기준 사이클` and `기준 리뷰 HEAD` — the segment's last cycle that is not a resolution, B — and the latest `베이스 판정` row's `베이스`, `충돌 파일` and `부류`. A resolution review writes its `cycle` row with `모드=해소` and `기준 사이클=<B's 사이클>`; the gate refuses it (exit 3, no row) unless `다음` reads `해소 리뷰` when it is written, and (exit 2, no row) unless B's row is that last non-resolution cycle, B is an ancestor of the row's `리뷰 HEAD`, the report's mode line reads `- **리뷰 모드**: 해소 (기준 사이클 <n>, 기준 리뷰 HEAD \`<B>\`, 베이스 \`<T>\`)` with the same B, T is a base some `베이스 판정` row of the segment named, `<리뷰 HEAD> ^B ^T` is not empty, and every merge in that set brings in only T or its history. A resolution cycle is not counted against `REIMPL_CYCLE_CAP`, and it is never a delta's basis; a resolution cycle that passes becomes the next R.

**On a routed run the CI layer lies on the same two merging values, read from the ledger alone.** `review_next[]` calls it on every snapshot, so it reads no network. H is the worktree tip, R the last cycle's `리뷰 HEAD`, `u`·`a`·`g` the `cc_ci_round` of `(seg, H)`, and V the latest `checks` row of `(seg, H)` with `재실행 회차` = `g`; 「open」 is `a` = `열림`, 「closed by `실패`」 is `a` = `실패`, and 「a settled `실패`」 is V.`상태` = `실패` with V.`진행 중` = 0. `CI_RERUN_CAP` and `CI_FIX_CAP` are 1. The first row that holds gives `다음`; the base rows sit at B:

| Order | Condition | `다음` | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| L0 | a `CI 처분` row with `처분=수정` follows the segment's last `cycle` row, H ≠ R and H's tree ≠ R's tree | `CI 리뷰` | the full review of H |
| B | B1–B3 above change the value | that base value | as above |
| L1 | open, and the top round's `처분` is `재실행` | `CI 재실행` | issue the act; it parks at `재실행요청실패` |
| L1l | open, and the top round's `처분` is `라벨` | `CI 라벨 인정` | issue the act; it parks at `라벨요청실패` |
| L2 | `g` = 0 and V is absent, `통과`, `미등록` or `판정 불가` — on a CI join target, `g` = 0 and V is `통과` or a `미등록` past the grace | the table's | base check, then merge |
| LW | a CI join target whose wait clock is past the wait cap, where L3 or L4 would give `CI 대기` | `CI 대기 한도` | park the segment |
| L3 | V = `대기`, or V = `실패` with `진행 중` > 0 | `CI 대기` | run other segments; end with `대기` carrying `'CI 대기=<id>'` |
| L4 | `g` ≥ 1 and V is absent, `미등록` or `판정 불가` — on a CI join target, any `g` with V absent, `판정 불가` or a `미등록` not past the grace | `CI 대기` | as L3 |
| L5 | `g` ≥ 1 and V = `통과` | the table's | base check, then merge |
| L5l | a settled `실패`, closed by `실패`, and the top round's `처분` is `라벨` | `CI 라벨 인정` | issue the act; it parks at `라벨요청실패` |
| L5m | a settled `실패`, `(seg, H)` has no `처분=라벨` row, and an element of V's `실패 체크` split on `, ` equals the `체크` of a `라벨 인정` row of the segment's target | `CI 라벨 인정` | `act --kind label-ack` |
| L6 | a settled `실패` and `u` < `CI_RERUN_CAP` | `CI 재실행` | `act --kind ci-rerun` |
| L6b | a settled `실패`, closed by `실패`, and the top round's `처분` is `재실행` | `CI 재실행` | issue the act; it parks at `재실행요청실패` (「already parked」 from the second same argv on) |
| L7 | a settled `실패`, `a` = `수락`, `u` ≥ the cap, and round `g`'s `요청` row reads `기본 브랜치=실패` | `CI 기존 실패` | the site `병합 거절` |
| L8 | a settled `실패`, `a` = `수락`, `u` ≥ the cap, and `f` < `CI_FIX_CAP` or `(seg, H)` has a `처분=수정` row | `CI 수정` | dispatch the implementation with `CI 수정: <H>` |
| L9 | otherwise | `CI 수정 한도` | the site `병합 거절` |

L0 comes before the base rows because the base overlay sends a head that moved past R to `해소 리뷰`, which would read a CI fix as a base resolution. Off a CI join target a tip with no `checks` row at round 0 keeps the table's value; on one it is `CI 대기`, because the merge rule refuses that head. `필수 집합` plays no part in the layer — required and non-required checks alike — while the poller still defers a `실패` while it reads `판정 불가`. L5l stands after V rather than at L1 so that a check a person fixed after a failed label round passes through L2 or L5 instead of holding the head for good. A label round is a round: it raises `g` and never `u`, so a rerun after an accepted label still has its one chance. At the nine CI values — `CI 재실행`, `CI 대기`, `CI 대기 한도`, `CI 수정`, `CI 합류 수정`, `CI 리뷰`, `CI 기존 실패`, `CI 수정 한도` and `CI 라벨 인정` — `review_next[]` also carries `head sha`, `PR`, `실패 체크`, `재실행 회차` and `기본 브랜치`, and at `CI 대기 한도` alone `상태` (V's, `관측 없음` when there is none) and `대기 시계` (UTC, `Z`).

**A CI join target is a segment of a routed run whose cutpoint is `PR`, `머지` or `배포`**, the cutpoint read for the alias `gate_segment_target_alias` gives; an empty alias or a cutpoint that does not resolve is not one. Every changed reading stands on this one predicate (`gate_ci_join_target`): the merge rule, both CI layers, the snapshot and the rerun act. Below `PR` no pull request is opened and no `checks` row comes, so nothing waits there. Three values are fixed in `liveness.sh`: the **grace** `CC_CI_UNREGISTERED_GRACE_SECONDS` = 300 — a round-0 `미등록` older than that, by its `관측`, means "this head has no CI" — and the **wait cap** `CC_CI_WAIT_CAP_SECONDS` = 7200. The **wait clock** of round `g` of `(seg, H)` is the first of these that parses: the `관측` of the round's earliest `checks` row; at `g` ≥ 1 the `관측` of the round's `수락` row; otherwise H's commit time in UTC — and once one of those set it, the latest `기록 시각` among the segment's `중단 답` rows a person gave replaces it when later (not a row whose `답한 주체` is `교대` or `좌석`, nor a row without that field whose `근거` opens with `자동 채택(구속-이탈) — `; a `기록 시각` that does not parse leaves out that row alone), so the time a segment spent waiting on a person at any halt is not counted against CI, and one answer buys one cap from its own time. A wait past the cap is `CI 대기 한도`, and the router parks the segment on it — every wait on a join target ends in a verdict, in "no CI", or there.

**On a CI join target a table `수정` carries the CI join layer** (`gate_ci_fix_next`), read the same way, so a review's findings and a failure that survived its rerun go back in one implement dispatch. `리드 수정` keeps the table's value. The first row that holds:

| Order | Condition | `다음` | Disposition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| M1 | `a` = `열림` or `실패` | `수정` | the review fix as today |
| M1n | `a` = `없음` — `(seg, H)` has no round | `수정` | the review fix as today, with no `CI 합류:` line |
| MW | the wait cap, where M2 would wait | `CI 대기 한도` | park the segment |
| M2 | V absent, `대기`, `실패` with `진행 중` > 0, `판정 불가`, or a `미등록` not past the grace | `CI 대기` | as L3 |
| M3 | V = `통과`, or round 0's `미등록` past the grace | `수정` | the review fix as today |
| M3a | a settled `실패`, `(seg, H)` has no `처분=라벨` row, and one element of V's `실패 체크` is the `체크` of a `라벨 인정` row of the segment's target (L5m's predicate) | `수정` | the review fix as today, with no `CI 합류:` line |
| M4 | a settled `실패` and `u` < `CI_RERUN_CAP` — past M1n only after an accepted label round | `수정` | the review fix as today |
| M5 | a settled `실패`, `a` = `수락`, and round `g`'s `요청` row reads `기본 브랜치=실패` | `수정` | the review fix as today |
| M6 | otherwise | `CI 합류 수정` | dispatch the review fix with `CI 합류: <H>` |

**The fix goes before any rerun or wait.** The fix commit moves the head and its CI starts again at round 0, so a rerun of the old head, or a wait on its result, buys a result with nowhere to go: M1n sends a head with no round straight to the fix whatever its CI shows, and past it M2 and MW wait only on an accepted round's result and M4 is reached only after an accepted label round. The layer never gives `CI 재실행`. M1, M1n, M3a, M4 and M5 carry no evidence that the segment's change can fix the failure, and the fix makes a new head whose CI starts again at round 0. M3a reads the predicate L5m reads (`gate_ci_ack_failure`): a failure of a check the manifest acknowledged is neither rerun nor handed to the implement stage as a CI fix, and the label path takes it at L5m on the new head once its review lets a merge through. It reaches the same `수정` M1 gives an open or failed label round. A tip that does not resolve leaves `수정`.

**The early rerun, E** (`gate_ci_early`), lets the one rerun go out while the segment's review still runs, since `다음` holds the table's value until that review ends. E holds on a CI join target that is neither `머지됨` nor `park`, with a live stage on the segment whose kind is a review, a tip that resolves, no round of either kind on `(seg, H)` (`max` = 0 — a label round raises `g` and never `u`, so `u` = 0 would read round 0's superseded V after an accepted label), round 0's V a settled `실패` none of whose `실패 체크` elements is the `체크` of a `라벨 인정` row of the segment's target (L5m's predicate, `gate_ci_ack_failure` — that failure is the label path's), and no `blocked` row with `사유=도달 park`, `세그먼트=<seg>` and `도달 판정` `재실행대상미상` or `재실행배포트리거` below the first `checks` row of `(seg, H)` — a rerun act that parks does so before its `요청` row, so without that clause E would hold forever. The snapshot's `ci_early[]` carries one element per segment where E holds: `세그먼트`, `head sha`, and round 0's `PR` and `실패 체크`; it is outside H and outside the default `--fields`. The relay's copy (`cc_ci_early_condition`) opens no manifest and so reads every clause but the acknowledged-check one: it is a superset, a shift it wakes for an acknowledged check finds `ci_early[]` empty and takes no act, and the consumption rule wakes it on that evidence once. The rerun act accepts when `다음` is `CI 재실행` or E holds; its other checks are unchanged.

**The routing path's dispatches are held to `다음`.** An implementation whose prompt carries `베이스 해소: <sha>` is refused (exit 3) unless `다음` is `베이스 해소` and the sha is the commit of the latest `베이스 판정` row's `베이스`; it does not spend the cycle budget. Any other implementation is refused (exit 3) once the segment has `REIMPL_CYCLE_CAP` `cycle` rows that are not resolutions, unless `다음` is `재구현`, or is `확인 리뷰` with a `정상` re-convergence row after the last `cycle` row, no lead-fix row after it and the segment's `implement_next[]` element reading `구현` — the same re-implementation's run after its process A —, or the prompt carries a `CI 수정: <sha>` line, `다음` as the gate recomputes it is `CI 수정` and the sha is the worktree tip; the refusal names `다음` as what to read instead.
